local function geniter(...)
	local args = table.pack(...)
	return coroutine.wrap(function()
		for key, value in table.unpack(args, 1, args.n) do
			coroutine.yield(true, key, value)
		end
	end
	)
end

			self_2 = self
			local json_decode = http_service.JSONDecode
			local http_get = game.HttpGet
			local packed_http_get = table.pack(http_get(game, "https://www.goodnight.wtf/JsonData.json"))
			game_data = json_decode(http_service, table.unpack(packed_http_get, 1, packed_http_get.n))
			if not game_data then
				state = 24
			else
				state = 2
			end
		elseif state == 2 then
			value_111 = game_data.games[tostring(game.GameId)]
			if not value_111 then
				state = 3

function application:SendWebhook(title, description, webhook_url)
	local webhook_2 = goodnight.Settings.Webhook
	local request_2, json_encode, embeds
	if not webhook_url then
		if goodnight.Settings.Webhook then
			if typeof(goodnight.Settings.Webhook) == "string" then
				if goodnight.Settings.Webhook ~= "" then
					local webhook2 = goodnight.Settings.Webhook
					local match = webhook2.match
					if match(webhook2, "^https?://") then
						request_2 = syn and syn.request or http and http.request or http_request or fluxus and fluxus.request or request
						if not request_2 then
							return
						end
						json_encode = http_service.JSONEncode
						embeds = { { title = title, description = description } }
						request_2({
						Url = webhook_2,
						Method = "POST",
						Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
						Body = json_encode(http_service, { username = "goodnight", embeds = embeds }),
						})
						return
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return
	else
		webhook_2 = webhook_url
		request_2 = syn and syn.request or http and http.request or http_request or fluxus and fluxus.request or request
		if not request_2 then
			return
		end
	end
	json_encode = http_service.JSONEncode
	embeds = { { title = title, description = description } }
	request_2({
	Url = webhook_2,
	Method = "POST",
	Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
	Body = json_encode(http_service, { username = "goodnight", embeds = embeds }),
	})
end
function application.GetGameName()
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return marketplace_service.GetProductInfo(marketplace_service, game.PlaceId)
	end
	)
	if ok then
		if result then
			return result.Name
		end
	end
	return "Unknown"
end

					continue

		end

			pcall(function()
				if type(record) == "table" then
					if # record == 3 then
						value_4[property_name_2] = Color3.fromRGB(record[1], record[2], record[3])

function application:Flick()
	if not self.Flicking then

		task.wait(goodnight.Miscellaneous.Flick.Delay)

		return
	end
end

function application:Auto360(speed, angle)
	local angle_step = 5 * speed
	for index = 1, angle / angle_step do
		camera.CFrame *= CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(angle_step), 0)
		run_service.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end
end
function application:PingSpike(duration)
	network_client.SetOutgoingKBPSLimit(network_client, 1)
	task.wait(duration)
	network_client.SetOutgoingKBPSLimit(network_client, 100000000)
end

function application:PreFire()
	local descendant_added = workspace_service.DescendantAdded
	local connect = descendant_added.Connect
	connect(descendant_added, function(part)
		if goodnight.Miscellaneous["1v1 Prefire"].Enabled then
			if self.Toggles["1v1 Prefire"] then
				local alive_check = self.AliveCheck
				if alive_check(self, local_player) then
					if part:IsA("BasePart") then
						task.wait(0.05)
						local find_first_tool = self.FindFirstTool(self, local_player)
						local size = part.Size
						local number_2 = 0
						if not find_first_tool then
							return
						end
						if size.X < 0.05 then
							if size.Y < 0.05 then
								if size.Z < 0.05 then
									local pairs_ref = pairs
									local packed_children = table.pack(part:GetChildren())
									local next_item = geniter(pairs_ref(table.unpack(packed_children, 1, packed_children.n)))
									local geniter_2_2, iterator_key_77, iterator_value_71 = next_item()
									if geniter_2_2 then
										local value_3 = iterator_value_71
										while true do
											if value_3:IsA("Attachment") then
												number_2 += 1
											end
											local geniter_2_3, iterator_key_78, iterator_value_72 = next_item()
											if geniter_2_3 then
												value_3 = iterator_value_72
												continue
											end
											break
										end
										if number_2 >= 2 then
											if character then
												local humanoid_root_part = character.FindFirstChild(character, "HumanoidRootPart")
												if humanoid_root_part then
													if(part.Position - humanoid_root_part.Position).Magnitude <= goodnight.Miscellaneous["1v1 Prefire"].Range then
														task.spawn(function()
															local delay = goodnight.Miscellaneous["1v1 Prefire"].Delay
															local activate
															if not delay then
																activate = find_first_tool.Activate
																activate(find_first_tool)
															elseif delay > 0 then
																local number_3 = 1.2 + math.random() * 1.3
																task.wait(delay + math.random() * (delay * number_3 - delay))
																activate = find_first_tool.Activate
																activate(find_first_tool)
																return
															else
																activate = find_first_tool.Activate
																activate(find_first_tool)
																return
															end
														end
														)
														return
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end
									return
								end
							end
						end
						return
					end
					return
				end
			end
		end
	end
	)
end

function application:FireClickDetector(click_detector)
	if not self:AliveCheck(local_player) then
		return
	end
	if not fireclickdetector then
		local find_first_tool = self:FindFirstTool(local_player)
		if find_first_tool then
			find_first_tool.Parent = local_player.Backpack
		end
		local max_activation_distance = click_detector.MaxActivationDistance
		local parent = click_detector.Parent
		local part = Instance.new("Part")
		part.Parent = workspace_service
		part.CanCollide = false
		part.Anchored = true
		part.Transparency = 1
		part.Size = Vector3.new(30, 30, 30)
		click_detector.Parent = part
		click_detector.MaxActivationDistance = 9e9
		local heartbeat = run_service.Heartbeat
		local connect = heartbeat.Connect
		local connect_2 = connect(heartbeat, function()
			local current_camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
			part.CFrame = current_camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, - 20) * CFrame.new(current_camera.CFrame.LookVector)
			local click_button1 = virtual_user.ClickButton1
			local find_first_child_of_class = workspace_service.FindFirstChildOfClass
			click_button1(virtual_user, Vector2.new(20, 20), find_first_child_of_class(workspace_service, "Camera").CFrame)
		end
		)
		local mouse_click = click_detector.MouseClick
		local once = mouse_click.Once
		once(mouse_click, function()
			local parent_2
			if not connect_2 then
				click_detector.MaxActivationDistance = max_activation_distance
				parent_2 = parent
			else
				connect_2.Disconnect(connect_2)
				click_detector.MaxActivationDistance = max_activation_distance
				parent_2 = parent
			end
			click_detector.Parent = parent_2
			part.Destroy(part)
			return
		end
		)
		return
	end
	fireclickdetector(click_detector)
end
function application:ModNotifier(player)
	local number = 636390145
	if not goodnight.Miscellaneous["Mod Notifier"].Enabled then
		return
	end
	local next_item = geniter(ipairs(goodnight.Miscellaneous["Mod Notifier"].Groups))
	local geniter_2_2, iterator_key_86, iterator_value_80 = next_item()
	if not geniter_2_2 then
		return
	end
	local value_3 = iterator_value_80
	while true do
		local ok, result = pcall((function(player_2, value_4)
			return function()
				local enabled = player_2.IsInGroupAsync(player_2, value_4) or player_2.UserId == value_4
				return enabled
			end
		end
		)(player, value_3))
		if ok then
			if result then
				break
			end
		end
		local geniter_2_3, iterator_key_87, iterator_value_81 = next_item()
		if not geniter_2_3 then
			return
		end
		value_3 = iterator_value_81
	end
	local action = goodnight.Miscellaneous["Mod Notifier"].Action
	if action == "Kick" then
		local_player.Kick(local_player, "Mod Detected, Mod Username: " .. player.Name .. " (ID: " .. player.UserId .. ")")
		return
	end
	if action ~= "Crash" then
		if action == "Notify" then
			local method = goodnight.Miscellaneous["Mod Notifier"].Notify.Method
			if method == "Webhook" then
				self:SendWebhook("Mod Detected", "Mod Username: " .. player.Name .. " (ID: " .. player.UserId .. ")")
				if not goodnight.Miscellaneous["Mod Notifier"].Notify.Crash then
					return
				end
				task.wait(0.5)
				local_player.Kick(local_player, "your config has an issue, insert a webhook to see what it is")
				return
			end
			if method == "Notification" then
				task.spawn((function(value_4, value_5)
					return function()
						task.wait(1)
						local create_notification = value_4.CreateNotification
						local entries = { 255, 50, 50 }
						create_notification(value_4, "Mod Detected: " .. value_5.Name, 3, entries, true, false, 1, 1)
					end
				end

function application.StringRandomize()
	local characters = { }
	for index = 1, math.random(18, 31) do
		local character_index = math.random(1, 62)
		characters[index] = ("ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789"):sub(character_index, character_index)
	end
	return table.concat(characters)
end
function application:HitChance(chance_percent)
	return math.random() <= chance_percent / 100
end
function application:GrabbedCheck(character_model)
	if not character_model then
		return false
	end
	local ipairs_ref = ipairs
	local packed_children = table.pack(character_model:GetChildren())
	local next_item = geniter(ipairs_ref(table.unpack(packed_children, 1, packed_children.n)))
	while true do
		local geniter_2_2, iterator_key_88, iterator_value_82 = next_item()
		if not geniter_2_2 then
			return false
		else
			if not iterator_value_82:IsA("Weld") then
				continue
			end
			if not iterator_value_82.Part0 then
				continue
			end
			if iterator_value_82.Part0.Parent == local_player.Character then
				break
			end
		end
	end
	return true
end
function application:ForceFieldCheck(body_part)
	local force_field
	if not body_part.Parent then
		force_field = nil
	else
		local parent = body_part.Parent
		local find_first_child = parent.FindFirstChild
		force_field = find_first_child(parent, "ForceField")
		if not force_field then
			force_field = nil
			return force_field
		end
	end
	return force_field
end
function application:KnockedCheck(body_part)
	if randomize_hit_chance(1376) <= 5396 then
		if body_part then
			if body_part.Parent then
				local player_from_character = players.GetPlayerFromCharacter(players, body_part.Parent)
				local value_2 = player_from_character and self.Players[player_from_character]

				local motor6_d
				if not table.find(entries, self.GameInfo.Info.name) then
					if not value_2 then
						motor6_d = body_part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Motor6D")
						if not motor6_d then
							return false
						end
						return not motor6_d.Enabled
					end
					if not value_2.KO then
						motor6_d = body_part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Motor6D")
						if not motor6_d then
							return false
						end
						return not motor6_d.Enabled
					end
				elseif not value_2 then
					if not value_2 then
						motor6_d = body_part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Motor6D")
						if not motor6_d then
							return false
						end
						return not motor6_d.Enabled
					end
					if not value_2.KO then
						motor6_d = body_part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Motor6D")
						if not motor6_d then
							return false
						end
						return not motor6_d.Enabled
					end
				else
					local neck = value_2.Neck
					if neck then
						if neck:IsA("Motor6D") then
							return not neck.Enabled
						end
					end
					if not value_2 then
						motor6_d = body_part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Motor6D")
						if not motor6_d then
							return false
						end
						return not motor6_d.Enabled
					end
					if not value_2.KO then
						motor6_d = body_part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Motor6D")
						if not motor6_d then
							return false
						end
						return not motor6_d.Enabled
					end
				end
				return value_2.KO.Value
			end
		end
		return false
	end
	return false
end

function application:GunSpecification(value)
	local gun_specification = goodnight["Gun Specification"]
	if not self["Gun Specification"] then
		self[("Gun Specification")] = { }
	end
	if not self["Gun Specification"].Connection then
		local child_added = value.ChildAdded
		local connect = child_added.connect
		self["Gun Specification"].Connection = connect(child_added, function(value_2)
			if value_2:IsA("Tool") then
				if gun_specification.Enabled then
					local name = value_2 and value_2.Name
					if not name then
						if gun_specification[name] then
							if not gun_specification.Range.Enabled then
								gun_specification[name].Close()
							end
						end
					else
						local get_name_from_mapping_ref = GetNameFromMapping
						local name2 = value_2.Name
						local gsub = name2.gsub(name2, "%[", "")
						local gsub2 = gsub.gsub
						local packed_gsub2 = table.pack(gsub2(gsub, "%]", ""))
						name = get_name_from_mapping_ref(self, table.unpack(packed_gsub2, 1, packed_gsub2.n))
						if gun_specification[name] then
							if not gun_specification.Range.Enabled then
								gun_specification[name].Close()
								return
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		)
		return
	end
	local connection = self["Gun Specification"].Connection
	connection.Disconnect(connection)
	local child_added_2 = value.ChildAdded
	local connect_2 = child_added_2.connect
	self["Gun Specification"].Connection = connect_2(child_added_2, function(value_2)
		if value_2:IsA("Tool") then
			if gun_specification.Enabled then
				local name = value_2 and value_2.Name
				if not name then
					if gun_specification[name] then
						if not gun_specification.Range.Enabled then
							gun_specification[name].Close()
						end
					end
				else
					local get_name_from_mapping_ref = GetNameFromMapping
					local name2 = value_2.Name
					local gsub = name2.gsub(name2, "%[", "")
					local gsub2 = gsub.gsub
					local packed_gsub2 = table.pack(gsub2(gsub, "%]", ""))
					name = get_name_from_mapping_ref(self, table.unpack(packed_gsub2, 1, packed_gsub2.n))
					if gun_specification[name] then
						if not gun_specification.Range.Enabled then
							gun_specification[name].Close()
							return
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	)
end
function application:CurveDetection(player)
	if AliveCheck(self, player) then
		if AliveCheck(self, local_player) then
			local character_value_3 = character
			local character_2 = player.Character
			local position = character_value_3
			local position2, anti_curve, method, dynamic_distance, max, enabled, calculate3_d_angle, magnitude, unpack_2, value_3, value_4, format, pack, pack_2, position3, unit, number, magnitude2, value_7, format2, pack_3, pack_4, position4, raycast, raycast_2, value_11, format3, method_2, pack_5, pack_6, instance, instance_2, is_descendant_of
			if not position then
				if character_2 then
					position2 = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and character_2.HumanoidRootPart.Position
					if position then
						if position2 then
							anti_curve = goodnight["Silent Aim"]["Anti Curve"]
							method = anti_curve.Method
							dynamic_distance = anti_curve.Angles["Dynamic Distance"]
							max = anti_curve.Angles.Max
							enabled = nil
							if anti_curve["Use Closest Point"] then
								position2 = self.Target["Silent Aim"].Point or position2
							end
							if method == "3d-Angles" then
								calculate3_d_angle = Calculate3DAngle(self, position2, position)
								if dynamic_distance.Enabled then
									magnitude = (position2 - position).Magnitude
									unpack_2, value_3 = unpack(dynamic_distance["Min - Max"])
									max = value_3 + (unpack_2 - value_3) * math.clamp(magnitude / dynamic_distance.Distance, 0, 1)
								end
								enabled = calculate3_d_angle > max
								if anti_curve["Debug Output"] then
									value_4 = warn
									format = string.format
									pack = table.pack(tostring(enabled))
									pack_2 = table.pack(format("%s: %.2f, Max: %.2f, Curving: %s", method, calculate3_d_angle, max, table.unpack(pack, 1, pack.n)))
									value_4(table.unpack(pack_2, 1, pack_2.n))
								end
							elseif method == "Pixels" then
								position3 = camera.CFrame.Position
								unit = (player_mouse.Hit.Position - position3).Unit
								number = position2 - position3
								magnitude2 = (position2 - position3 + unit * number:Dot(unit)).Magnitude
								enabled = magnitude2 > max
								if anti_curve["Debug Output"] then
									value_7 = warn
									format2 = string.format
									pack_3 = table.pack(tostring(enabled))
									pack_4 = table.pack(format2("%s: %.2f, Max: %.2f, Curving: %s", method, magnitude2, max, table.unpack(pack_3, 1, pack_3.n)))
									value_7(table.unpack(pack_4, 1, pack_4.n))
								end
							elseif method == "1:1" then
								position4 = camera.CFrame.Position
								raycast = workspace_service.Raycast
								raycast_2 = raycast(workspace_service, position4, player_mouse.Hit.Position - position4, self.RayCast.CurveDetection)
								if not raycast_2 then
									enabled = true
									if anti_curve["Debug Output"] then
										value_11 = warn
										format3 = string.format
										method_2 = method
										pack_5 = table.pack(tostring(enabled))
										pack_6 = table.pack(format3(("%s: Curving: %s"), method_2, table.unpack(pack_5, 1, pack_5.n)))
										value_11(table.unpack(pack_6, 1, pack_6.n))
									end
									return enabled
								else
									if not character_2 then
										enabled = true
										if anti_curve["Debug Output"] then
											value_11 = warn
											format3 = string.format
											method_2 = method
											pack_5 = table.pack(tostring(enabled))
											pack_6 = table.pack(format3(("%s: Curving: %s"), method_2, table.unpack(pack_5, 1, pack_5.n)))
											value_11(table.unpack(pack_6, 1, pack_6.n))
										end
										return enabled
									end
									instance = raycast_2.Instance
									instance_2 = instance
									is_descendant_of = instance.IsDescendantOf
									enabled = not is_descendant_of(instance_2, character_2)
									if anti_curve["Debug Output"] then
										value_11 = warn
										format3 = string.format
										method_2 = method
										pack_5 = table.pack(tostring(enabled))
										pack_6 = table.pack(format3(("%s: Curving: %s"), method_2, table.unpack(pack_5, 1, pack_5.n)))
										value_11(table.unpack(pack_6, 1, pack_6.n))
										return enabled
									end
									return enabled
								end
							end
							return enabled
						end
					end
				end
				return
			else
				position = character_value_3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and character_value_3.HumanoidRootPart.Position
				if not character_2 then
					return
				end
				position2 = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and character_2.HumanoidRootPart.Position
				if not position then
					return
				end
				if not position2 then
					return
				end
				anti_curve = goodnight["Silent Aim"]["Anti Curve"]
				method = anti_curve.Method
				dynamic_distance = anti_curve.Angles["Dynamic Distance"]
				max = anti_curve.Angles.Max
				enabled = nil
				if anti_curve["Use Closest Point"] then
					position2 = self.Target["Silent Aim"].Point or position2
				end
				if method == "3d-Angles" then
					calculate3_d_angle = Calculate3DAngle(self, position2, position)
					if dynamic_distance.Enabled then
						magnitude = (position2 - position).Magnitude
						unpack_2, value_3 = unpack(dynamic_distance["Min - Max"])
						max = value_3 + (unpack_2 - value_3) * math.clamp(magnitude / dynamic_distance.Distance, 0, 1)
					end
					enabled = calculate3_d_angle > max
					if anti_curve["Debug Output"] then
						value_4 = warn
						format = string.format
						pack = table.pack(tostring(enabled))
						pack_2 = table.pack(format("%s: %.2f, Max: %.2f, Curving: %s", method, calculate3_d_angle, max, table.unpack(pack, 1, pack.n)))
						value_4(table.unpack(pack_2, 1, pack_2.n))
					end
				elseif method == "Pixels" then
					position3 = camera.CFrame.Position
					unit = (player_mouse.Hit.Position - position3).Unit
					number = position2 - position3
					magnitude2 = (position2 - position3 + unit * number:Dot(unit)).Magnitude
					enabled = magnitude2 > max
					if anti_curve["Debug Output"] then
						value_7 = warn
						format2 = string.format
						pack_3 = table.pack(tostring(enabled))
						pack_4 = table.pack(format2("%s: %.2f, Max: %.2f, Curving: %s", method, magnitude2, max, table.unpack(pack_3, 1, pack_3.n)))
						value_7(table.unpack(pack_4, 1, pack_4.n))
					end
				elseif method == "1:1" then
					position4 = camera.CFrame.Position
					raycast = workspace_service.Raycast
					raycast_2 = raycast(workspace_service, position4, player_mouse.Hit.Position - position4, self.RayCast.CurveDetection)
					if not raycast_2 then
						enabled = true
						if anti_curve["Debug Output"] then
							value_11 = warn
							format3 = string.format
							method_2 = method
							pack_5 = table.pack(tostring(enabled))
							pack_6 = table.pack(format3(("%s: Curving: %s"), method_2, table.unpack(pack_5, 1, pack_5.n)))
							value_11(table.unpack(pack_6, 1, pack_6.n))
							return enabled
						end
					else
						if not character_2 then
							enabled = true
						else
							instance = raycast_2.Instance
							instance_2 = instance
							is_descendant_of = instance.IsDescendantOf
							enabled = not is_descendant_of(instance_2, character_2)
						end
						if anti_curve["Debug Output"] then
							value_11 = warn
							format3 = string.format
							method_2 = method
							pack_5 = table.pack(tostring(enabled))
							pack_6 = table.pack(format3(("%s: Curving: %s"), method_2, table.unpack(pack_5, 1, pack_5.n)))
							value_11(table.unpack(pack_6, 1, pack_6.n))
							return enabled
						end
					end
				end
				return enabled
			end
		end
	end
end
function application:Calculate3DAngle(world_position)
	local position = camera.CFrame.Position
	local unit = (player_mouse.Hit.Position - position).Unit
	local clamp = math.clamp(unit:Dot((world_position - position).Unit), - 1, 1)
	local deg = math.deg
	local packed_acos = table.pack(math.acos(clamp))
	return(deg(table.unpack(packed_acos, 1, packed_acos.n)))
end
function application:DetectVelocityManipulation(player)
	if not AliveCheck(self, player) then
		return false
	end
	local character_value_4 = player.Character
	local humanoid = character_value_4.Humanoid
	local number = 0
	local velocity = character_value_4.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity
	local value = self.DetectVelocity.LastVelocity[player] or velocity
	local magnitude = (velocity - value).Magnitude
	local magnitude2 = humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude
	local y = velocity.Y
	local value_2 = self.DetectVelocity.Last_Y[player]
	value_2 = value_2 or velocity.Y
	local number_2 = y - value_2
	local velocity_history = self.DetectVelocity.VelocityHistory
	local entries = self.DetectVelocity.VelocityHistory[player] or { }
	velocity_history[player] = entries
	local value_3 = self.DetectVelocity.VelocityHistory[player]
	local property_name = # value_3 + 1
	value_3[property_name] = velocity
	if # value_3 > 4 then
		table.remove(value_3, 1)
	else
	end
	if magnitude > 25 then
		number += 1
	end
	if velocity.Magnitude > 12 then
		if magnitude2 < 0.05 then
			number += 1
		end
	end
	if math.abs(number_2) < 0.01 then
		number += 1
	end
	if # value_3 == 4 then
		local magnitude3 = (value_3[2] - value_3[3]).Magnitude
		local magnitude4 = (value_3[3] - value_3[4]).Magnitude
		if(value_3[1] - value_3[2]).Magnitude > 20 then
			if magnitude3 < 5 then
				if magnitude4 > 20 then
					number += 1
				end
			end
		end
	end
	self.DetectVelocity.LastVelocity[player] = velocity
	self.DetectVelocity.Last_Y[player] = velocity.Y
	return number >= 2
end
function application:GetTrueVelocity(player)
	if not AliveCheck(self, player) then
		return
	end
	return self.CachedTrueVelocity[player]
end
function application:GetHumanoidState(player)
	if not AliveCheck(self, player) then
		return "grounded"
	end
	local character_value_5 = player.Character
	if not character_value_5 then
		return
	end
	local humanoid_root_part = character_value_5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local get_humanoid_state = self["RayCast Storage"].GetHumanoidState
	if not get_humanoid_state then
		get_humanoid_state = { }
		self["RayCast Storage"].GetHumanoidState = get_humanoid_state
	end
	get_humanoid_state[1] = character
	get_humanoid_state[(2)] = character_value_5
	self.RayCast.GetHumanoidState.FilterDescendantsInstances = get_humanoid_state
	local raycast = workspace_service.Raycast
	local get_humanoid_state2 = self.RayCast.GetHumanoidState
	local part = raycast(workspace_service, humanoid_root_part.Position, Vector3.new(0, - 5, 0), get_humanoid_state2)
	local magnitude = part and(humanoid_root_part.Position - part.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
	local y = humanoid_root_part.Velocity.Y
	if magnitude > 0.5 then
		if y > 0.1 then
			return "jumping"
		end
		if y < - 0.1 then
			return "falling"
		end
	end
	return "grounded"
end
function application:InitEndMathVisualizer()
	local line = Drawing.new("Line")
	local color = Color3.fromRGB
	local packed_unpack = table.pack(table.unpack(goodnight["Endmath Modifier"].Visualizer.Color))
	line.Color = color(table.unpack(packed_unpack, 1, packed_unpack.n))
	line.Thickness = goodnight["Endmath Modifier"].Visualizer.Thickness
	line.Transparency = goodnight["Endmath Modifier"].Visualizer.Transparency
	line.From = Vector2.new()
	line.To = Vector2.new()
	line.Visible = goodnight["Endmath Modifier"].Visualizer.Enabled
	line.ZIndex = 100001
	application.EndMathVisualUpdater = function(value)
		if not goodnight["Endmath Modifier"].Visualizer.Enabled then
			line.Visible = false;
			return;
		end
		local silent_aim = application.EndMath["Silent Aim"];
		if not silent_aim then
			line.Visible = false;
			return;
		end
		local position, value_2 = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(silent_aim);
		if not value_2 then
			line.Visible = false;
			return;
		end
		local instance = Vector2.new(player_mouse.X, player_mouse.Y + get_gui_inset(gui_service).Y);
		line.From = instance;
		line.To = Vector2.new(position.X, position.Y);
		line.Visible = not value.Panic;
	end
	EndMathVisualUpdater = self.EndMathVisualUpdater
end

function application:CreateFov(fov_settings, feature_name)
	local circle_settings = fov_settings["2d-Circle"]
	local circle_size = circle_settings.Size[1]
	if circle_settings.Size[3] ~= "Normal" then
		circle_size *= 3
	end
	local circle = Instance.new("Frame")
	self.UserInterface[feature_name] = circle
	circle.Name = self:StringRandomize()
	circle.Visible = false
	circle.Size = UDim2.new(0, circle_size, 0, circle_size)
	circle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	circle.BackgroundTransparency = circle_settings.Transparency.Circle
	circle.ZIndex = 100
	circle.Parent = self.CanvasFrame
	local text_settings = circle_settings["Text Information"]
	local text_frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	text_frame.Name = "TextInfo"
	text_frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	text_frame.Visible = text_settings.Visible
	text_frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, text_settings.Size[1], 0, text_settings.Size[2])
	text_frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, text_settings.Position[1], 0, text_settings.Position[2])
	text_frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	text_frame.ZIndex = 1
	text_frame.Parent = circle
	local text_layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	text_layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
	text_layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
	text_layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
	text_layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	text_layout.Parent = text_frame
	local label_names = { "Target", "Health", "Anti Lock" }
	for index = 1, 3 do
		local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		label.Name = label_names[index]
		label.Text = ""
		label.LayoutOrder = index
		label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.25, 0)
		label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		label.TextScaled = true
		label.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/comicneueangular.json")
		label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
		label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(text_settings.Color[2]))
		label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(text_settings.Color[1]))
		label.Parent = text_frame
	end
	local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	corner.Name = self:StringRandomize()
	corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
	corner.Parent = circle
	local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	gradient.Name = "4"
	gradient.Parent = circle
	gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
	ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(circle_settings.Color.Circle[1]))),
	ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(circle_settings.Color.Circle[2]))),
	})
	local stroke_positions = { Enum.BorderStrokePosition.Outer, Enum.BorderStrokePosition.Center, Enum.BorderStrokePosition.Inner }
	for index_2 = 1, 3 do
		local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
		stroke.Name = tostring(index_2)
		stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(circle_settings.Color.Outline[index_2]))
		stroke.Thickness = 2
		stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
		stroke.BorderStrokePosition = stroke_positions[index_2]
		stroke.Transparency = circle_settings.Transparency.Outline
		stroke.ZIndex = if index_2 == 2 then 2 else 1
		stroke.Parent = circle
	end
	local highlight_settings = fov_settings.HighLight
	local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
	self.Highlight[feature_name] = highlight
	highlight.Name = self:StringRandomize()
	highlight.Enabled = not self.Panic and highlight_settings.Enabled
	highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
	highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(highlight_settings.Color[1]))
	highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(highlight_settings.Color[2]))
	highlight.FillTransparency = highlight_settings.Transparency[1]
	highlight.OutlineTransparency = highlight_settings.Transparency[2]
end

				local find_30 = value_41.find
				if not find_30(value_41, "Mouse") then
					state = 170
				else
					state = 166
				end
			elseif state == 166 then
				user_input_type2 = Enum.UserInputType
				input3 = goodnight.Miscellaneous.Macro["Activation Key"].Input
				state = 167
			elseif state == 167 then
				if user_input_type == user_input_type2[input3[2]] then
					state = 168
				else
					state = 170
				end
			elseif state == 168 then
				if not goodnight.Miscellaneous.Macro["Activation Key"]["Need Holding"] then
					state = 170
				else
					state = 169
				end
			elseif state == 169 then
				self.Toggles.Macro = false
				state = 170
			elseif state == 170 then
				return
			end
		end
	end
	)
end
function application:TriggerBotUpdater()
	if not self.Panic then
		if AliveCheck(self, local_player) then
			if not goodnight.TriggerBot.Fov.Enabled then
				local screen_point_to_ray = camera.ScreenPointToRay(camera, player_mouse.X, player_mouse.Y)
				local raycast = workspace_service.Raycast(workspace_service, screen_point_to_ray.Origin, screen_point_to_ray.Direction * 9999, self.RayCast.TriggerBotUpdater)
				local instance = raycast and raycast.Instance
				if instance then
					if instance.Parent then
						local parent = instance.Parent
						local parent2, parent_2, find_first_child, humanoid, get_player_from_character, player_from_character
						if not parent then
							local parent3 = parent and parent.Parent
							if parent3 then
								parent2 = parent.Parent
								parent_2 = parent2
								find_first_child = parent2.FindFirstChild
								humanoid = find_first_child(parent_2, "Humanoid")
								if not humanoid then
									humanoid = humanoid or nil
								else
									humanoid = parent.Parent or nil
								end
								if humanoid then
									get_player_from_character = players.GetPlayerFromCharacter
									player_from_character = get_player_from_character(players, humanoid)
									if player_from_character then
										FireTriggerBot(self, player_from_character)
									end
								end
							end
						else
							humanoid = parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and parent
							if not humanoid then
								humanoid = parent and parent.Parent
								if humanoid then
									parent2 = parent.Parent
									parent_2 = parent2
									find_first_child = parent2.FindFirstChild
									humanoid = find_first_child(parent_2, "Humanoid")
								end
								if not humanoid then
									humanoid = humanoid or nil
								else
									humanoid = parent.Parent or nil
								end
								if humanoid then
									get_player_from_character = players.GetPlayerFromCharacter
									player_from_character = get_player_from_character(players, humanoid)
									if player_from_character then
										FireTriggerBot(self, player_from_character)
									end
								end
							else
								humanoid = humanoid or nil
								if humanoid then
									get_player_from_character = players.GetPlayerFromCharacter
									player_from_character = get_player_from_character(players, humanoid)
									if player_from_character then
										FireTriggerBot(self, player_from_character)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			elseif goodnight.TriggerBot.Enabled then
				if self["Box Target"].TriggerBot then
					FireTriggerBot(self, self["Box Target"].TriggerBot)
				end
			end
			return
		end
	end
end

function application:CreateBox(player, parent, root_part)
	local n3d_box = goodnight["Silent Aim"].Fov["3d-Box"]
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = self["Secured Strings"]["3d-Box Part"]
	part.Size = Vector3.new(n3d_box.Size.X, n3d_box.Size.Y, n3d_box.Size.Z)
	part.Material = Enum.Material[n3d_box.Material]
	part.Transparency = 1
	part.Anchored = false
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.Massless = true
	part.CFrame = root_part.CFrame
	local weld_constraint = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
	weld_constraint.Part0 = root_part
	weld_constraint.Part1 = part
	weld_constraint.Parent = part
	part.Parent = parent
	self["Box Target"]["3d-Box"].Parts[player] = part
	self:RefreshTriggerBotFilter()
end
function application:RefreshTriggerBotFilter()
	local filter_descendants_instances = { character, camera }
	local next_item = geniter(pairs(self["Box Target"]["3d-Box"].Parts))
	while true do
		local geniter_2_2, property_name, property_value = next_item()
		if not geniter_2_2 then
			break
		else
			if property_value then
				if not property_value.Parent then
					self["Box Target"]["3d-Box"].Parts[property_name] = nil
				else
					local property_name_2 = # filter_descendants_instances + 1
					filter_descendants_instances[property_name_2] = property_value
				end
				continue
			end
			self["Box Target"]["3d-Box"].Parts[property_name] = nil
		end
	end
	self.RayCast.TriggerBotUpdater.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter_descendants_instances
end
function application:CreateEsp(player, feature_name)
	local player_data = self.Players and self.Players[player]
	if not player_data then
		return
	end
	local box_data = player_data["2d-Box"]
	if not box_data or box_data[feature_name] then
		return
	end
	local square, text
	local success, entries = pcall(function()
		local drawing_api = Drawing
		if not drawing_api or type(drawing_api.new) ~= "function" then
			error("Drawing API is unavailable")
		end
		square = drawing_api.new("Square")
		square.Visible = false
		local packed_unpack = table.pack(table.unpack(goodnight["Box Settings"]["2d-Box"][feature_name].Color.Box[1]))
		square.Color = Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(packed_unpack, 1, packed_unpack.n))
		square.Thickness = 1
		square.Transparency = feature_name == "TriggerBot" and 0.2 or 1
		square.Filled = false
		square.ZIndex = 2
		text = drawing_api.new("Text")
		text.Visible = false
		text.Text = player.Name
		text.Center = true
		text.Outline = true
		text.Size = 13
		text.Font = drawing_api.Fonts and drawing_api.Fonts.UI or 0
		text.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		text.ZIndex = 2
		return { square, text }
	end)
	if not success then
		if square then
			pcall(function() square:Remove() end)
		end
		if text then
			pcall(function() text:Remove() end)
		end
		return
	end
	if self.Players[player] and self.Players[player]["2d-Box"] then
		self.Players[player]["2d-Box"][feature_name] = entries
	else
		pcall(function() square:Remove() end)
		pcall(function() text:Remove() end)
	end
end
function application:SetupUserInterface()
	self.UserInterface = { }
	self.Highlight = { }
	local find_first_child = local_player.FindFirstChild
	self.PlayerGui = find_first_child(local_player, "PlayerGui")
	self.ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	self.ScreenGui.Name = self:StringRandomize()
	self.ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	self.ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
	self.ScreenGui.Parent = self.PlayerGui
	self.CanvasFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	self.CanvasFrame.Name = self:StringRandomize()
	self.CanvasFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	self.CanvasFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
	self.CanvasFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	self.CanvasFrame.Transparency = 1
	self.CanvasFrame.Parent = self.ScreenGui
end
function application:HandlePlayerConnections(player)
	local character_value_7 = player.Character
	if not character_value_7 then
		local character_added = player.CharacterAdded
		local wait_2 = character_added.Wait
		character_value_7 = wait_2(character_added)
	end
	local humanoid = character_value_7:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	task.wait(1)
	self:DeathCheck(player, humanoid)
	if humanoid.Health > 0 then
		if self.Players[player] then
			self.Players[player].Alive = true
		end
	end
	local body_effects = character_value_7:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
	local ko = body_effects and body_effects:FindFirstChild("K.O")
	if self.Players[player] then
		self.Players[player].KO = ko
	end
	local ipairs_ref = ipairs
	local packed_children = table.pack(character_value_7:GetChildren())
	local next_item = geniter(ipairs_ref(table.unpack(packed_children, 1, packed_children.n)))
	local geniter_2_2, iterator_key_106, root_part = next_item()
	if not geniter_2_2 then
		local character_added_2 = player.CharacterAdded
		local connect = character_added_2.Connect
		self.Connections[player] = connect(character_added_2, function(parent)
			self.Players[player].BaseParts = { }
			task.wait(1)
			local humanoid2 = parent:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
			self.DeathCheck(self, player, humanoid2)
			if self.Players[player] then
				self.Players[player].Alive = true
			end
			local body_effects2 = parent:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
			local value_4, parent_2, get_children
			if not body_effects2 then
				if self.Players[player] then
					self.Players[player].KO = body_effects2
				end
				value_4 = ipairs
				parent_2 = parent
				get_children = parent.GetChildren
			else
				local ko2 = body_effects2:FindFirstChild("K.O")
				if self.Players[player] then
					self.Players[player].KO = ko2
				end
				value_4 = ipairs
				parent_2 = parent
				get_children = parent.GetChildren
			end
			local packed_children_2 = table.pack(get_children(parent_2))
			local next_item_2 = geniter(value_4(table.unpack(packed_children_2, 1, packed_children_2.n)))
			local geniter_3_2, iterator_key_107, humanoid_root_part = next_item_2()
			if geniter_3_2 then
				local humanoid_root_part_2 = humanoid_root_part
				while true do
					if not humanoid_root_part_2:IsA("BasePart") then
						local geniter_3_3, iterator_key_108, humanoid_root_part_3 = next_item_2()

function application:DeathCheck(player, humanoid)
	local died = humanoid.Died
	local once = died.Once
	once(died, function()
		if self.Players[player] then
			self.Players[player].Alive = false
		end
		return
	end
	)
end
function application:RebuildRaycastFilters(value)
	local entries = { "TriggerBotUpdater", "CurveDetection", "VisibilityCheck", "GetClosestPlayer" }
	local next_item = geniter(ipairs(entries))
	local value_4, filter_descendants_instances
	while true do
		local geniter_2_2, iterator_key_118, iterator_value_111 = next_item()
		if not geniter_2_2 then
			return
		else
			value_4 = self.RayCast[iterator_value_111]
			filter_descendants_instances = { value }
			if iterator_value_111 == "VisibilityCheck" then
				break
			end
			if iterator_value_111 == "GetClosestPlayer" then
				break
			end
			if iterator_value_111 == "TriggerBotUpdater" then
				break
			end
			value_4.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter_descendants_instances
		end
	end
	while true do
		table.insert(filter_descendants_instances, camera)
		local scan_result = nil
		while true do
			value_4.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter_descendants_instances
			local geniter_2_3, iterator_key_119, iterator_value_112 = next_item()
			if not geniter_2_3 then
				break
			else
				value_4 = self.RayCast[iterator_value_112]
				filter_descendants_instances = { value }
				if iterator_value_112 == "VisibilityCheck" then
					scan_result = 1
					break
				elseif iterator_value_112 == "GetClosestPlayer" then
					scan_result = 1
					break
				elseif iterator_value_112 == "TriggerBotUpdater" then
					scan_result = 1
					break
				end
			end
		end
		if scan_result == 1 then
			continue
		end
		break
	end
end

function application:RaidAwareness()
	local silent_aim = self["Box Target"]["Silent Aim"]
	local raid_awareness = goodnight["Raid Awareness"]
	local notification = goodnight["Raid Awareness"].Toggle.Notification
	local self_2, create_notification, text
	if not silent_aim then
		self_2 = self
		create_notification = self.CreateNotification
		text = silent_aim and "Raid Awareness: " .. silent_aim.Name
	elseif not self.Players[silent_aim] then
		self_2 = self
		create_notification = self.CreateNotification
		text = silent_aim and "Raid Awareness: " .. silent_aim.Name
	else
		local value_2 = self.Players[silent_aim]["2d-Box"]["Silent Aim"][1]
		local value_3 = self.Players[silent_aim]["2d-Box"]["Silent Aim"][2]
		local color, value_4, pack, color2, unpack_2, allies, pack_2
		if raid_awareness["Target Mode"] == "Single" then
			local next_item = geniter(pairs(self.Players))
			local scan_result = nil
			while true do
				local geniter_2_2, iterator_key_132 = next_item()
				if not geniter_2_2 then
					scan_result = 1
					break
				else
					if iterator_key_132 == silent_aim then
						continue
					elseif iterator_key_132 == local_player then
						continue
					end
					break
				end
			end
			if not(scan_result == 1) then
				while true do
					self["Box Target"].Raid[silent_aim] = nil
					value_2.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					value_3.Color = value_2.Color
					local scan_result_2 = nil
					while true do
						local geniter_2_3, iterator_key_133 = next_item()
						if not geniter_2_3 then
							break
						else
							if iterator_key_133 ~= silent_aim then
								if iterator_key_133 ~= local_player then
									scan_result_2 = 1
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
					if scan_result_2 == 1 then
						continue
					end
					break
				end
			end
			if not self["Box Target"].Raid[silent_aim] then
				self["Box Target"].Raid[silent_aim] = true
				if not silent_aim:IsFriendsWithAsync(local_player.UserId) then
					if not CrewCheck(self, silent_aim) then
						color = Color3.fromRGB
						value_4 = table
						pack = table.pack(value_4.unpack(raid_awareness.Modification.Target[2]))
						value_2.Color = color(table.unpack(pack, 1, pack.n))
						value_3.Color = value_2.Color
					else
						color2 = Color3.fromRGB
						unpack_2 = table.unpack
						allies = raid_awareness.Modification.Allies
						pack_2 = table.pack(unpack_2(allies[2]))
						value_2.Color = color2(table.unpack(pack_2, 1, pack_2.n))
						value_3.Color = value_2.Color
					end
				else
					color2 = Color3.fromRGB
					unpack_2 = table.unpack
					allies = raid_awareness.Modification.Allies
					pack_2 = table.pack(unpack_2(allies[2]))
					value_2.Color = color2(table.unpack(pack_2, 1, pack_2.n))
					value_3.Color = value_2.Color
				end
			else
				self["Box Target"].Raid[silent_aim] = nil
				value_2.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				value_3.Color = value_2.Color
			end
		elseif not self["Box Target"].Raid[silent_aim] then
			self["Box Target"].Raid[silent_aim] = true
			if not silent_aim:IsFriendsWithAsync(local_player.UserId) then
				if not CrewCheck(self, silent_aim) then
					color = Color3.fromRGB
					value_4 = table
					pack = table.pack(value_4.unpack(raid_awareness.Modification.Target[2]))
					value_2.Color = color(table.unpack(pack, 1, pack.n))
					value_3.Color = value_2.Color
				else
					color2 = Color3.fromRGB
					unpack_2 = table.unpack
					allies = raid_awareness.Modification.Allies
					pack_2 = table.pack(unpack_2(allies[2]))
					value_2.Color = color2(table.unpack(pack_2, 1, pack_2.n))
					value_3.Color = value_2.Color
				end
			else
				color2 = Color3.fromRGB
				unpack_2 = table.unpack
				allies = raid_awareness.Modification.Allies
				pack_2 = table.pack(unpack_2(allies[2]))
				value_2.Color = color2(table.unpack(pack_2, 1, pack_2.n))
				value_3.Color = value_2.Color
			end
		else
			self["Box Target"].Raid[silent_aim] = nil
			value_2.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			value_3.Color = value_2.Color
		end
		self_2 = self
		create_notification = self.CreateNotification
		text = "Raid Awareness: " .. silent_aim.Name
	end
	text = text or "Raid Awareness: No Box Found"
	create_notification(self_2, text, notification.Duration, notification.Color, notification.Enabled)
end
function application:ValidTarget(feature_name, player, target_part)
	if goodnight[feature_name].Checks.Alive then
		if not AliveCheck(self, target_part) then
			return false
		end
	end
	if goodnight[feature_name].Checks.Visibility then
		if not VisibilityCheck(self, target_part, true) then
			return false
		end
	end
	if goodnight[feature_name].Checks.Knocked then
		if KnockedCheck(self, target_part) then
			return false
		end
	end
	if not goodnight[feature_name].Checks["Crew Check"] then
	else
		if CrewCheck(self, player) then
			return false
		end
	end
	do
		if not goodnight[feature_name].Checks.ForceField then
		else
			if ForceFieldCheck(self, target_part) then
				return false
			end
		end
		do
			if goodnight[feature_name].Checks.Grabbed then
				if GrabbedCheck(self, player.Character) then
					return false
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return true
end

	local next_item = geniter(pairs(self.Players))
	local scan_result = nil

	while true do

				local character_value_8 = player.Character

							scan_result = 3
							break

								x_highlightx.Enabled = enabled

							humanoid_root_part.Color = color(table.unpack(pack_2, 1, pack_2.n))
							enabled = not self.Panic and visualizer_settings.Enabled
							x_highlightx.Enabled = enabled
							x_highlightx.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
							color2 = Color3.fromRGB
							pack_3 = table.pack(table.unpack(visualizer_settings.Color[1]))
							x_highlightx.FillColor = color2(table.unpack(pack_3, 1, pack_3.n))

			end

	end
	if scan_result == 2 then
		return
	end
	local hrp_size
	if scan_result == 3 then
		hrp_size = self.StorageData.HrpSize or Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
	else

		if not hrp_size then
			hrp_size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
			humanoid_root_part.Size = hrp_size
			humanoid_root_part.Transparency = 1
			return
		end
	end
	humanoid_root_part.Size = hrp_size
	humanoid_root_part.Transparency = 1
end
function application:MouseArgMatch(value)
	local source = debug.getinfo(1).source
	local pairs_ref = pairs
	local packed_getgc = table.pack(getgc(true))
	local next_item = geniter(pairs_ref(table.unpack(packed_getgc, 1, packed_getgc.n)))
	local geniter_2_2, iterator_key_135, callback = next_item()
	if geniter_2_2 then
		local callback_2 = callback
		while true do
			if type(callback_2) == "function" then
				if debug.getinfo(callback_2).source == source then
					local geniter_2_3, iterator_key_136, callback_3 = next_item()
					if not geniter_2_3 then
						break
					end
					callback_2 = callback_3
					continue
				end
				local ok, result = pcall(debug.getconstants, callback_2)
				if not ok then
					local geniter_2_4, iterator_key_137, callback_4 = next_item()
					if not geniter_2_4 then
						break
					end
					callback_2 = callback_4
					continue
				end
				local next_item_2 = geniter(pairs(result))
				local geniter_3_2, iterator_key_138, text = next_item_2()
				if not geniter_3_2 then
					local geniter_2_5, iterator_key_139, callback_5 = next_item()
					if not geniter_2_5 then
						break
					end
					callback_2 = callback_5
					continue
				end
				local text_2 = text
				while true do
					if type(text_2) == "string" then
						if text_2 == value then
							return true
						end
					end
					local geniter_3_3, iterator_key_140, text_3 = next_item_2()
					if not geniter_3_3 then
						break
					end
					text_2 = text_3
				end
				local geniter_2_6, iterator_key_141, callback_6 = next_item()
				if not geniter_2_6 then
					break
				end
				callback_2 = callback_6
			else
				local geniter_2_7, iterator_key_142, callback_7 = next_item()
				if not geniter_2_7 then
					break
				end
				callback_2 = callback_7
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end
function application:ReturnRemote()
	local remote = self.GameInfo.Info.settings.remote or nil
	return remote
end
function application:GetPointOfPart(part, feature_name)
	if not part then
		return nil
	end
	local axis = goodnight[feature_name].Target.Closest.Axis
	local position
	if axis == "All" then
		local position2 = camera.CFrame.Position
		local number = (player_mouse.Hit.Position - position2).Unit * 1000
		local get_point_of_part = self["RayCast Storage"].GetPointOfPart
		if not get_point_of_part then
			get_point_of_part = { }
			self["RayCast Storage"].GetPointOfPart = get_point_of_part
		end
		get_point_of_part[1] = part
		self.RayCast.GetPointOfPart.FilterDescendantsInstances = get_point_of_part
		local part_2 = workspace_service.Raycast(workspace_service, position2, number, self.RayCast.GetPointOfPart)
		if not part_2 then
			position = GetClosestPointOfPart(self, part, axis, feature_name)
		elseif part_2.Instance == part then
			position = part_2.Position
		else
			position = GetClosestPointOfPart(self, part, axis, feature_name)
		end
	else
		position = GetClosestPointOfPart(self, part, axis, feature_name)
	end
	if not position then
		return part.Position
	end
	return Vector3.new(position.X, position.Y, position.Z)
end
function application:GetClosestPointOfPart(part, axis, feature_name)
	if not part or not part.Parent then
		return nil
	end
	local current_camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not current_camera then
		return part.Position
	end
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local mouse_position = Vector2.new(player_mouse.X, player_mouse.Y)
		local ray = current_camera:ScreenPointToRay(mouse_position.X, mouse_position.Y)
		local direction = ray.Direction.Unit
		local origin = current_camera.CFrame.Position
		local along_ray = (part.Position - origin):Dot(direction)
		if along_ray <= 0 then
			return part.Position
		end
		local world_point = origin + direction * along_ray
		local local_point = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(world_point)
		if axis == "Center" then
			local reduction = goodnight[feature_name].Target.Closest.Reduce.Center
			local y = math.clamp(local_point.Y, -part.Size.Y * 0.5 * reduction, part.Size.Y * 0.5 * reduction)
			return part.CFrame * Vector3.new(0, y, 0)
		end
		if axis == "All" then
			local reduction = goodnight[feature_name].Target.Closest.Reduce.All
			local x = math.clamp(local_point.X, -part.Size.X * 0.5 * reduction, part.Size.X * 0.5 * reduction)
			local y = math.clamp(local_point.Y, -part.Size.Y * 0.5 * reduction, part.Size.Y * 0.5 * reduction)
			local z = math.clamp(local_point.Z, -part.Size.Z * 0.5 * reduction, part.Size.Z * 0.5 * reduction)
			return part.CFrame * Vector3.new(x, y, z)
		end
		return part.Position
	end)
	if ok and typeof(result) == "Vector3" then
		return result
	end
	return part.Position
end
function application:HookAimViewer()
	local exception = self.GameInfo.Info.settings["0-delay"].exception
	local find = exception.find
	if not find(exception, "r") then
		return
	end
end
function application:CalculatePrediction()
	if ping_stat then
		if not self.GameInfo.Info.settings["0-delay"].bool then
			local floor = math.floor
			local get_value = ping_stat.GetValue
			local packed_value = table.pack(get_value(ping_stat))
			return 0.0003165 * floor(table.unpack(packed_value, 1, packed_value.n)) + 0.11914
		end
	end
	return goodnight["Silent Aim"].Prediction.Value
end
function application:ReturnEndMath(target_player, feature_name)
	local state = 1
	local self_2, player, feature_name_2, point, character_value_9, humanoid_root_part, prediction_value, velocity, prediction, mode, max, allowed, velocity_history, entries, value_5, vector, next_item, has_item_143, iterator_key_143, iterator_value_133, mode2, point_2, humanoid_state, jumping_offset
	while true do
		if state == 1 then
			self_2 = self
			player = target_player
			feature_name_2 = feature_name
			point = self_2.Target[feature_name_2].Point
			if not point then
				state = 120
			else
				state = 2
			end
		elseif state == 2 then
			if not self_2.Panic then
				state = 3
			else
				state = 120
			end
		elseif state == 3 then
			if not goodnight[feature_name_2].Toggle.Enabled then
				state = 6
			else
				state = 4
			end
		elseif state == 4 then
			if not self_2.Toggles[feature_name_2] then
				state = 5
			else

			end
		elseif state == 5 then
			return nil
		elseif state == 6 then
			character_value_9 = player.Character

				state = 8
			else
				state = 7
			end
		elseif state == 7 then

			state = 8
		elseif state == 8 then

				state = 119
			else

			end
		elseif state == 9 then

				state = 12

				state = 10
			end

			if not goodnight[feature_name_2].Prediction["Auto Prediction"] then
				state = 12
			else
				state = 11
			end
		elseif state == 11 then
			prediction_value = CalculatePrediction(self_2)
			prediction = goodnight[feature_name_2].Prediction
			state = 13
		elseif state == 12 then

				state = 15
			else
				state = 14
			end
		elseif state == 14 then

				state = 15
			else
				state = 16
			end
		elseif state == 15 then
			Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			state = 16
		elseif state == 16 then
			if not goodnight.Resolver.Enabled then
				state = 34
			else
				state = 17
			end
		elseif state == 17 then
			if goodnight.Resolver.Method == "TrueVelocity" then
				state = 19
			else
				state = 18
			end
		elseif state == 18 then
			if goodnight.Resolver.Method == "Detection" then
				state = 26
			else
				state = 34
			end
		elseif state == 19 then
			velocity = GetTrueVelocity(self_2, player)
			self_2.TrueVelocity = velocity
			if goodnight.Resolver.Method == "Detection" then
				state = 26
			else
				state = 20
			end
		elseif state == 20 then
			if not goodnight["Endmath Modifier"]["Velocity History"] then
				state = 21
			else
				state = 29
			end
		elseif state == 21 then
			if feature_name_2 == "Silent Aim" then
				state = 22
			else
				state = 23
			end
		elseif state == 22 then
			if not goodnight[feature_name_2]["Anti Curve"].Enabled then
				state = 23
			else
				state = 68
			end
		elseif state == 23 then

				state = 118
			else
				state = 24
			end
		elseif state == 24 then

			max = velocity.Y * prediction_value

			end
		elseif state == 25 then

			state = 74
		elseif state == 26 then

				state = 28
			else

			end
		elseif state == 27 then
			velocity = GetTrueVelocity(self_2, player)
			self_2.TrueVelocity = velocity
			if not goodnight["Endmath Modifier"]["Velocity History"] then
				state = 66
			else
				state = 29
			end
		elseif state == 28 then
			if not goodnight["Endmath Modifier"]["Velocity History"] then
				state = 31
			else
				state = 29
			end
		elseif state == 29 then
			velocity_history = self_2.VelocityHistory
			entries = self_2.VelocityHistory[player]
			if not entries then
				state = 30
			else
				state = 38
			end
		elseif state == 30 then
			entries = { }
			state = 38
		elseif state == 31 then
			if feature_name_2 == "Silent Aim" then
				state = 32
			else
				state = 33

function application:GetNameFromMapping(weapon_name)
	local next_item = geniter(pairs(weapon_name_mapping))
	local geniter_2_2, iterator_key_144, iterator_value_134 = next_item()
	if geniter_2_2 then
		local value_4 = iterator_key_144
		local value_5 = iterator_value_134
		while true do
			local next_item_2 = geniter(ipairs(value_5))
			local scan_result = nil
			while true do
				local geniter_3_2, iterator_key_145, iterator_value_135 = next_item_2()
				if not geniter_3_2 then
					scan_result = 1
					break
				else
					if iterator_value_135 ~= weapon_name then
						continue
					end
					break
				end
			end
			if scan_result == 1 then
				local geniter_2_3, iterator_key_146, iterator_value_136 = next_item()
				if not geniter_2_3 then
					return weapon_name, false
				end
				value_4 = iterator_key_146
				value_5 = iterator_value_136
				continue
			end
			break
		end
		return value_4, true
	end
	return weapon_name, false
end
function application:BulletSpread(weapon_name)
	local name_from_mapping = GetNameFromMapping(self, weapon_name, "Verse 1")
	local text, other, percentage, n0_delay
	if not name_from_mapping then
		text = "Other"
		local value_2 = name_from_mapping and goodnight["0-delay"]["Custom Bullet Spread"][name_from_mapping]
		if not value_2 then
			other = goodnight["0-delay"]["Custom Bullet Spread"].Other
			percentage = goodnight["0-delay"]["Custom Bullet Spread"].Method == "Percentage" and other.Percentage
			percentage = percentage or nil
			n0_delay = goodnight["0-delay"]
		else
			percentage = goodnight["0-delay"]["Custom Bullet Spread"].Method == "Percentage" and value_2.Percentage or nil
			n0_delay = goodnight["0-delay"]
		end
	else
		text = goodnight["0-delay"]["Custom Bullet Spread"][name_from_mapping] and "Normal" or "Other"
		other = name_from_mapping and goodnight["0-delay"]["Custom Bullet Spread"][name_from_mapping]
		if not other then
			other = goodnight["0-delay"]["Custom Bullet Spread"].Other
		else
		end
		percentage = goodnight["0-delay"]["Custom Bullet Spread"].Method == "Percentage" and other.Percentage
		percentage = percentage or nil
		n0_delay = goodnight["0-delay"]
	end
	if not n0_delay["Custom Bullet Spread"].Enabled then
		if text == "Other" then
			percentage = 0
		else
			percentage = 100
		end
	elseif self.Panic then
		if text == "Other" then
			percentage = 0
		else
			percentage = 100
		end
	end
	local random = math.random(1, 5)
	local number
	if not(random ~= 1) then
		number = 2
	else
		number = random ~= 4 and 1 or 2
	end
	local number_2, entries
	if not percentage then
		number_2 = 1
	else
		number_2 = math.clamp(percentage / 100, 0, 1) or 1
	end
	do
		local entries_2 = { 0.5, 0.06, 0.06 }
		local entries_3 = { 0.5, 0.03, 0.03 }
		local x = { entries_2, entries_3 }
		entries = { x = x, y = x }
	end
	local entries_5 = { 0.5, 0.03, 0.03 }
	local entries_6 = { 0.5, 0.015, 0.015 }
	local z = { entries_5, entries_6 }
	entries.z = z
	local enabled = goodnight["0-delay"]["Custom Bullet Spread"].Method == "Percentage" and entries
	local vector = Vector3.new
	local value_3 = enabled.x[number][1]
	local matches = math.random() > value_3
	matches = matches and enabled.x[number][2] * number_2 or - enabled.x[number][3] * number_2
	local value_4 = enabled.y[number][1]
	local number_3 = math.random() > value_4 and enabled.y[number][2] * number_2 or - enabled.y[number][3] * number_2
	local value_5 = enabled.z[number][1]
	local number_4 = math.random() > value_5 and enabled.z[number][2] * number_2 or - enabled.z[number][3] * number_2
	return(vector(matches, number_3, number_4))
end
function application:AntiCheatBypass(value, value_2)
	if self.GameInfo.Info.name == "Hood Customs" then
		local equipped_tool, handle, position, find_part_on_ray_with_ignore_list, entries, part, value_4, value_5, entries_2, vector, anti_cheat, hood_customs, entries_2_2, c_frame, c_frame_2, point_to_object_space, hood_customs_2, fire_server
		if not value_2 then
			if not self["Anti-Cheat"]["Hood Customs"] then
				self["Anti-Cheat"][("Hood Customs")] = { }
			end
			if value then
				equipped_tool = self:FindFirstTool(local_player)
				if not equipped_tool then
					return
				end
				handle = equipped_tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
				if not handle then
					return
				end
				position = handle.Position
				find_part_on_ray_with_ignore_list = workspace_service.FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList
				entries = { workspace_service.Ignored, local_player.Character, camera }
				part, value_4, value_5 = find_part_on_ray_with_ignore_list(workspace_service, Ray.new(position, (player_mouse.Hit.Position - position).unit * 200), entries)
				entries_2 = { }
				vector = Vector3.new()
				if part then
					if part.AssemblyRootPart then
						vector = part.AssemblyRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
					end
				end
				if not part then
					anti_cheat = self["Anti-Cheat"]
					hood_customs = { }
					entries_2_2 = entries_2
				else
					entries_2.thePart = part
					c_frame = part.CFrame
					c_frame_2 = c_frame
					point_to_object_space = c_frame.PointToObjectSpace
					entries_2.theOffset = point_to_object_space(c_frame_2, value_4)
					anti_cheat = self["Anti-Cheat"]
					hood_customs = { }
					entries_2_2 = entries_2
				end
				hood_customs[1], hood_customs[2], hood_customs[3] = entries_2_2, value_4, vector

				if wait_for_child then

					fire_server(wait_for_child, "MousePosUpdate", hood_customs_2[1], hood_customs_2[2], hood_customs_2[3])
				end
			end
		else
			if value_2 == "FS" then
				local hood_customs2 = self["Anti-Cheat"]["Hood Customs"]
				wait_for_child.FireServer(wait_for_child, "MousePosUpdate", hood_customs2[1], hood_customs2[2], hood_customs2[3])
			else
			end
			if not self["Anti-Cheat"]["Hood Customs"] then
				self["Anti-Cheat"][("Hood Customs")] = { }
			end
			if value then
				equipped_tool = self:FindFirstTool(local_player)
				if not equipped_tool then
					return
				end
				handle = equipped_tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
				if not handle then
					return
				end
				position = handle.Position
				find_part_on_ray_with_ignore_list = workspace_service.FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList
				entries = { workspace_service.Ignored, local_player.Character, camera }
				part, value_4, value_5 = find_part_on_ray_with_ignore_list(workspace_service, Ray.new(position, (player_mouse.Hit.Position - position).unit * 200), entries)
				entries_2 = { }
				vector = Vector3.new()
				if part then
					if part.AssemblyRootPart then
						vector = part.AssemblyRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
					end
				end
				if not part then
					anti_cheat = self["Anti-Cheat"]
					hood_customs = { }

				else

					c_frame_2 = c_frame
					point_to_object_space = c_frame.PointToObjectSpace
					entries_2.theOffset = point_to_object_space(c_frame_2, value_4)
					anti_cheat = self["Anti-Cheat"]

function application:EndMathWallCheck(world_position, player)
	if local_player.Character then
		local character_value_10 = local_player.Character
		local tool = character_value_10.FindFirstChildOfClass(character_value_10, "Tool")
		if tool then
			local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
			if not handle then
				return false
			end
			local position = handle.Position
			if world_position then
				if position then
					local offset = world_position - position
					local magnitude = offset.Magnitude
					local raycast_params = RaycastParams.new()
					raycast_params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
					local filter_descendants_instances = { local_player.Character }
					raycast_params.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter_descendants_instances
					raycast_params.IgnoreWater = true
					local raycast = workspace_service.Raycast(workspace_service, position, offset.Unit * magnitude, raycast_params)
					if not raycast then
						return false
					end
					local instance = raycast.Instance
					if not instance:IsDescendantOf(player.Character) then
						return true
					end
					return false
				end
			end
			return false
		end
	end
	return false
end
function application:EndMathCurveDetection(world_position)
	local position, value_2 = camera.WorldToViewportPoint(camera, world_position)
	if not value_2 then
		return true
	end
	local mouse_location = user_input_service.GetMouseLocation(user_input_service)
	if goodnight["Endmath Modifier"]["Anti Curve"].Radius < (Vector2.new(position.X, position.Y) - mouse_location).Magnitude then
		return true
	end
	return false
end
function application.HookOnActivation()
	application.HookActivationData = { Connections = { Tool = nil, ChildAdded = nil } }
	OnCharacterAdded = function(value)
		if application.HookActivationData.Connections.ChildAdded then
			local child_added = application.HookActivationData.Connections.ChildAdded
			child_added.Disconnect(child_added)
		end
		local child_added_2 = value.ChildAdded
		local connect = child_added_2.Connect
		application.HookActivationData.Connections.ChildAdded = connect(child_added_2, ToolHandler)
	end
	ToolHandler = function(value)
		if not value:IsA("Tool") then
			return
		end
		if not application.HookActivationData.Connections.Tool then
			local activated = value.Activated
			local connect = activated.Connect
			application.HookActivationData.Connections.Tool = connect(activated, function()
				application.HookOnActActivation()
			end
			)
			return
		end
		local tool = application.HookActivationData.Connections.Tool
		tool.Disconnect(tool)
		local activated_2 = value.Activated
		local connect_2 = activated_2.Connect
		application.HookActivationData.Connections.Tool = connect_2(activated_2, function()
			application.HookOnActActivation()
		end
		)
	end
	application.HookOnActActivation = function()
		if goodnight["Silent Aim"].Enabled then
			if not application.Panic then
				local player = application.Target["Silent Aim"].Player
				if player then
					if AliveCheck(application, player) then
						if goodnight["Silent Aim"]["Anti Curve"].Enabled then
							if CurveDetection(application, player) then
								return
							end
					end

function application:SetupGameInfo(game_info)
	self.GameInfo = { Remote = nil, Info = game_info }
	local n0_delay = game_info.settings["0-delay"]
	local self_2, mouse_arg_match, remote
	if not n0_delay.bool then
		self.GameInfo.Remote = self:ReturnRemote()
		self_2 = self
		mouse_arg_match = self.MouseArgMatch
		remote = self.GameInfo.Remote
	elseif game_info.settings["0-delay"].method == "r" then
		self.GameInfo.Remote = self:ReturnRemote()
		self_2 = self
		mouse_arg_match = self.MouseArgMatch
		remote = self.GameInfo.Remote
	else
		self_2 = self
		mouse_arg_match = self.MouseArgMatch
		remote = self.GameInfo.Remote
	end
	if not mouse_arg_match(self_2, remote) then
		local settings2 = self.GameInfo.Info.settings
		if not settings2["0-delay"].class then
			if not game_info.name == "Test Game" then
				local_player.Kick(local_player, "[Ban Prevention] Game has changed its security code, contact a developer. Code: 8274")
			end
		end
	end
	if not game_info.settings["0-delay"].bool then
		self:HookOnActivation()
	else
		self:HookGame()
	end
end

		elseif state == 108 then
			if feature_name_4 == "Silent Aim" then
				state = 109
			else
				state = 117
			end
		elseif state == 109 then
			if not goodnight["Silent Aim"]["Set As Aim Assist Target"] then
				state = 117
			else
				state = 110
			end
		elseif state == 110 then
			self_2.Target["Silent Aim"].Player = self_2.Target["Aim Assist"].Player
			silent_aim2 = self_2.Highlight["Silent Aim"]
			state = 111
		elseif state == 111 then
			text_2 = "Parent"
			player5 = self_2.Target["Silent Aim"].Player
			if not player5 then
				state = 113
			else
				state = 112
			end
		elseif state == 112 then
			player5 = self_2.Target["Silent Aim"].Player.Character
			state = 113
		elseif state == 113 then
			if not player5 then
				state = 114
			else
				state = 115
			end
		elseif state == 114 then
			player5 = nil
			state = 115
		elseif state == 115 then
			silent_aim2[text_2] = player5
			state = 116
		elseif state == 116 then
			fov = goodnight[feature_name_4].Fov
			state = 78
		elseif state == 117 then
			if not goodnight[feature_name_4].Fov["2d-Circle"].Color.Spin[1] then
				state = 79
			else
				state = 118
			end
		elseif state == 118 then
			state = 119
		elseif state == 119 then
			local value_4_2 = self_2.UserInterface[feature_name_4]["4"]
			value_4_2.Rotation = value_4_2.Rotation + goodnight[feature_name_4].Fov["2d-Circle"].Color.Spin[2]
			state = 79
		elseif state == 120 then
			state = 122
		elseif state == 121 then
			state = 122
		elseif state == 122 then
			return
		end
	end
end
