function application:GetBoxMath(body_part, screen_position, feature_name)
	local size = goodnight["Box Settings"]["2d-Box"][feature_name].Size
	local number = body_part.Size.Y * camera.ViewportSize.Y / screen_position.Z * 2 * 0.70020753820970971 / math.tan(math.rad(camera.FieldOfView) / 2)
	local number_2 = size.Width * number
	local number_3 = size.Height * number
	local vector2 = Vector2.new(number_2, number_3)
	return number_2, number_3, Vector2.new(screen_position.X - number_2 / 2, screen_position.Y - number_3 / 2), vector2
end
function application:GetClosestPart(player)
	local player_data = self.Players[player]
	if not player_data then
		return nil, "UpperTorso"
	end
	local body_parts = player_data.BaseParts
	local raycast_params = RaycastParams.new()
	raycast_params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
	raycast_params.FilterDescendantsInstances = body_parts
	local mouse_ray = camera:ScreenPointToRay(player_mouse.X, player_mouse.Y)
	local hit = workspace_service:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, mouse_ray.Direction * 1000, raycast_params)
	if hit and hit.Instance:IsA("BasePart") and hit.Instance.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
		return hit.Instance, hit.Instance.Name
	end
	local closest_part
	local closest_distance = math.huge
	local mouse_position = Vector2.new(player_mouse.X, player_mouse.Y)
	for _, body_part in ipairs(body_parts) do
		if body_part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
			local screen_position, visible = camera:WorldToScreenPoint(body_part.Position)
			if visible then
				local distance = (Vector2.new(screen_position.X, screen_position.Y) - mouse_position).Magnitude
				if distance < closest_distance then
					closest_part = body_part
					closest_distance = distance
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return closest_part, closest_part and closest_part.Name or "UpperTorso"
end
function application:GetClosestPlayer(feature_name, fov_mode)
	local player = nil
	local huge = math.huge
	if fov_mode == "2d-Circle" then
		if feature_name == "Silent Aim" then
			if goodnight[feature_name].HitChance[1] then
				if not HitChance(self, goodnight[feature_name].HitChance[2]) then
					return player
				end
			end
		end
		local iterator, value_4, value_5 = pairs(self.Players)
		for player_2 in value_4, value_5 do
			if AliveCheck(self, player_2) then
				local part = GetClosestPart(self, player_2)
				if part then
					local position, value_6 = camera:WorldToScreenPoint(part.Position)
					local magnitude = (Vector2.new(position.X, position.Y) - Vector2.new(player_mouse.X, player_mouse.Y)).Magnitude
					if value_6 then
						if not(self.UserInterface[feature_name].Size.X.Offset / 2 < magnitude) then
							if self:ValidTarget(feature_name, player_2, part) then
								if magnitude < huge then
									huge = magnitude
									player = player_2
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	else
		if fov_mode == "2d-Box" then
			if feature_name == "Silent Aim" then
				return self["Box Target"][feature_name]
			end
		end
		if fov_mode == "3d-Box" then
			if feature_name == "Silent Aim" then
				local screen_point_to_ray = camera:ScreenPointToRay(player_mouse.X, player_mouse.Y)
				local hit = workspace_service:Raycast(screen_point_to_ray.Origin, screen_point_to_ray.Direction * 9999, self.RayCast.GetClosestPlayer)
				local instance = hit and hit.Instance or nil
				if instance then
					if instance.Name == self["Secured Strings"]["3d-Box Part"] then
						local player_from_character = players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(instance.Parent)
						if not player_from_character then
							return nil
						end
						local character_value_13 = player_from_character.Character
						if not character_value_13 then
							return nil
						end
						local humanoid_root_part = character_value_13:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if not humanoid_root_part then
							return nil
						end
						if self:ValidTarget(feature_name, player_from_character, humanoid_root_part) then
							return player_from_character
						end
						return nil
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return player
end
function application:ClosestMode(feature_name)
	local player = self.Target[feature_name].Player
	local base_part = goodnight[feature_name].Target.BasePart
	if player then
		if AliveCheck(self, player) then
			if not goodnight[feature_name].Target.Closest.Enabled then
				if player.Character[base_part] then
					self.Target[feature_name].Part = player.Character[base_part]
					self.Target[feature_name].Point = self.Target[feature_name].Part.Position
					return
				end
			end
			local target = self.Target[feature_name]
			local target_2 = goodnight[feature_name].Target
			local closest_part, value_3 = GetClosestPart(self, player)
			target.Part = closest_part
			target_2.BasePart = value_3
			local pos = goodnight[feature_name].Target.Closest.Method:find("Point")
			local target_3, position
			if not pos then
				target_3 = self.Target[feature_name]
				position = self.Target[feature_name].Part and self.Target[feature_name].Part.Position or nil
				target_3.Point = position
			elseif not self.Target[feature_name].Part then
				target_3 = self.Target[feature_name]
				position = self.Target[feature_name].Part and self.Target[feature_name].Part.Position or nil
				target_3.Point = position
			else
				self.Target[feature_name].Point = GetPointOfPart(self, self.Target[feature_name].Part, feature_name)
			end
		else
			self.Target[feature_name].Point = nil
		end
	else
		self.Target[feature_name].Point = nil
	end
	local text_info = self.UserInterface[feature_name].TextInfo
	local text_information = goodnight[feature_name].Fov["2d-Circle"]["Text Information"]
	local player2 = self.Target[feature_name].Player
	local visible = text_information.Visible
	if visible then
		visible = player2
	end
	text_info.Visible = visible
	if text_info.Visible then
		text_info.Size = UDim2.new(text_information.Size[1], 0, text_information.Size[2], 0)
		text_info.Position = UDim2.new(text_information.Position[1], 0, text_information.Position[2], 0)
		local player3 = self.Target[feature_name].Player
		local player_2 = player3
		if player_2 then
			player_2 = AliveCheck(self, self.Target[feature_name].Player)
		end
		if player_2 then
			player_2 = player3
		end
		player_2 = player_2 or ""
		local name = player3
		if name then
			name = player_2.Name
		end
		name = name or "No One"
		local tostring_ref = tostring
		local floor = math.floor
		local health = player3 and player_2.Character.Humanoid.Health or 0
		local packed_floor = table.pack(floor(health))
		local text = "Health: " .. tostring_ref(table.unpack(packed_floor, 1, packed_floor.n))
		local tostring_ref_2 = tostring
		local enabled
		if player3 then
			table.pack(table.unpack(packed_floor, 1, packed_floor.n))
			enabled = DetectVelocityManipulation(self, player_2) or false
		else
			table.pack(table.unpack(packed_floor, 1, packed_floor.n))
			enabled = false
		end
		local text_2 = "AntiLock: " .. tostring_ref_2(enabled)
		local iterator, value_5, value_6 = ipairs({ "Target", "Health", "Anti Lock" })
		for value_, value_7 in value_5, value_6 do
			if text_info[value_7] then
				text_info[value_7].Visible = text_information.Configuration[value_7]
				text_info[value_7].TextTransparency = text_information.Transparency
				local value_8 = text_info[value_7]
				local stroke = self.Colors["Silent Aim 2d-Circle Text Information"].Stroke
				text_info[value_7].TextColor3 = self.Colors["Silent Aim 2d-Circle Text Information"].Main
				value_8.TextStrokeColor3 = stroke
				local value_9 = text_info[value_7]
				local matches = value_7 == "Target"
				if matches then
					matches = name
				end
				if not matches then
					matches = value_7 == "Health"
					if matches then
						matches = text
					end
				end
				if not matches then
					matches = value_7 == "Anti Lock"
					if matches then
						matches = text_2
					end
				end
				value_9.Text = matches
			end
		end
	end
end
function application:HandleAimAssist()
	local aim_assist = goodnight["Aim Assist"];
	if not aim_assist.Enabled then
		return;
	end
	if not AliveCheck(self, local_player) then
		return;
	end
	local find_first_tool = FindFirstTool(self, local_player);
	if find_first_tool and string.upper(find_first_tool.Name):find("KNIFE") then
		return;
	end
	ClosestMode(self, "Aim Assist");
	local aim_assist_2 = self.EndMath["Aim Assist"];
	local aim_assist_3 = self.Target["Aim Assist"];
	local player = aim_assist_3.Player;
	if not find_first_tool and aim_assist_3 and goodnight.Settings["Performance Mode"].Enabled and goodnight.Settings["Performance Mode"].ActiveGun then
		goodnight.Settings["Performance Mode"].ActiveGun = false;
	end
	local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head");
	if not head then
		return;
	end
	if head.LocalTransparencyModifier == 1 then
		if not goodnight["Aim Assist"].Checks["First Person"] then
			return;
		end
	else
		if not goodnight["Aim Assist"].Checks["Third Person"] then
			return;
		end
	end
	if not aim_assist_2 or not player or not AliveCheck(self, player) then
		return;
	end
	local apply_shake = aim_assist["Apply Shake"];
	local axis = apply_shake.Axis;
	local o = apply_shake.Randomized and math.random(- axis[1], axis[1]) or axis[1];
	local m = apply_shake.Randomized and math.random(- axis[2], axis[2]) or axis[2];
	local z = apply_shake.Randomized and math.random(- axis[3], axis[3]) or axis[3];
	local p = camera.CFrame.p;
	local g = apply_shake.Enabled and aim_assist_2 + Vector3.new(o, m, z) * 0.1 or aim_assist_2;
	local value = aim_assist.Smoothness[1] and aim_assist.Smoothness[2] or 0.1;
	local checks = aim_assist.Checks;
	local part = aim_assist.EasingStyle;
	local m_2 = true;
	if goodnight["Aim Assist"].Checks.Tool and not find_first_tool then
		m_2 = false;
	end
	if checks.Visibility and not VisibilityCheck(self, player.Character.RightUpperArm, true) then
		return;
	end
	if checks.Knocked and(KnockedCheck(self, player.Character.RightUpperArm) or character and character.RightUpperArm and KnockedCheck(self, character.RightUpperArm)) then
		aim_assist_3.Player = nil;
		self.Highlight["Aim Assist"].Parent = nil;
		return;
	end
	if m_2 then
		if aim_assist["Follow Mode"] == "Camera" then
			if aim_assist.Smoothness[1] then
				camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.new(p, g), value, Enum.EasingStyle[part.Position.In], Enum.EasingStyle[part.Position.Out], Enum.EasingStyle[part.Rotation.In], Enum.EasingStyle[part.Rotation.Out]);
			else
				camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(p, g);
			end
		else
			local position, value_2 = camera:WorldToScreenPoint(g);
			if not value_2 then
				return;
			end
			mousemoverel((position.X - player_mouse.X) * value, (position.Y - player_mouse.Y) * value);
		end
	end
end
function application:AliveCheck(player)
	if player == local_player then
		return character ~= nil and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") ~= nil;
	end
	local player_data = self.Players[player];
	return player_data ~= nil and player_data.Alive or false;
end
function application:OnScreen(world_position)
	return select(2, camera:WorldToScreenPoint(world_position));
end
function application:FindFirstTool(player)
	if not AliveCheck(self, player) or not character then
		return nil;
	end
	return character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool");
end
function application:VisibilityCheck(body_part, skip_screen_check)
	local position = camera.CFrame.Position;
	if not skip_screen_check then
		if not OnScreen(self, body_part.Position) then
			return false;
		end
	end
	local raycast = workspace_service:Raycast(position, body_part.Position - position, self.RayCast.VisibilityCheck);
	return not raycast or raycast.Instance:IsDescendantOf(body_part.Parent);
end
function application:BoxUpdater(feature_name)
	local fov, value_2 = goodnight[feature_name].Fov, goodnight["Box Settings"]["2d-Box"][feature_name];
	local player, huge = nil, math.huge;
	local prediction_value = goodnight[feature_name].Prediction.Value;
	local mouse_x = self.FrameCache.mouse_x;
	local mouse_y = self.FrameCache.mouse_y;
	for player_2, player_data in pairs(self.Players) do
		local box_data = player_data and player_data["2d-Box"];
		local drawing_pair = box_data and box_data[feature_name];
		local drawing = drawing_pair and drawing_pair[1];
		local drawing_2 = drawing_pair and drawing_pair[2];
		if not drawing or not drawing_2 then
			continue;
		end
		if feature_name == "Silent Aim" and(goodnight["Silent Aim"].Fov.Mode == "2d-Box" or goodnight["Raid Awareness"].Enabled or goodnight.Miscellaneous.Esp.Enabled) or feature_name == "TriggerBot" and goodnight.TriggerBot.Fov.Enabled then
			if drawing.Visible then
				drawing.Visible = false;
				drawing_2.Visible = false;
			end
			if not AliveCheck(self, player_2) then
				continue;
			end
			local part = player_data.HumanoidRootPart;
			local head = player_data.Head;
			if not part or not head then
				continue;
			end
			local position = part.Position;
			if humanoid_root and part then
				local position_2 = part.Position;
				local position_3 = humanoid_root.Position;
				local position_4 = position_2 - position_3;
				local c_3 = position_4.X * position_4.X + position_4.Y * position_4.Y + position_4.Z * position_4.Z;
				local value_5 = (value_2["Max Distance"] or 150) ^ 2;
				if c_3 > value_5 then
					continue;
				end
			end
			if value_2.Checks.Knocked and KnockedCheck(self, head) then
				continue;
			end
			if value_2.Checks["Crew Check"] and CrewCheck(self, player_2) then
				continue;
			end
			if goodnight["Box Settings"]["2d-Box"][feature_name]["Predicted Position"] then
				position = (position + part.Velocity * prediction_value);
			end
			local position_2_2, value_6 = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(position);
			if not value_6 then
				continue;
			end
			if goodnight.Miscellaneous.Esp.Enabled and self.Toggles.Esp then
				if goodnight.Miscellaneous.Esp.VisibleCheck and value_2.Checks.WallCheck and not VisibilityCheck(self, part, true) then
					continue;
				end
			else
				if value_2.Checks.WallCheck and not VisibilityCheck(self, part, true) then
					continue;
				end
			end
			local c_frame = part.CFrame;
			local box_math, value_7, position_5, size = GetBoxMath(self, part, position_2_2, feature_name);
			local transparency = value_2.Extras.Transparency;
			local filled = value_2.Extras.Filled;
			local k_2 = feature_name == "Silent Aim";
			drawing.Transparency = transparency;
			drawing.Size = size;
			drawing.Position = position_5;
			drawing.Filled = filled;
			if value_2.Name[1] then
				drawing_2.Text = player_2[value_2.Name[2]];
				drawing_2.Size = math.clamp(value_7 / 15, 13, 16);
				drawing_2.Position = position_5 + Vector2.new(box_math / 2, - drawing_2.TextBounds.Y - 6);
			end
			local x = position_2_2.X;
			local y = position_2_2.Y;
			local l_3 = box_math / 2;
			local value_8 = value_7 / 2;
			local value_9 = x - l_3;
			local c_7 = x + l_3;
			local m_6 = y - value_8;
			local l_4 = y + value_8;
			if mouse_x >= value_9 and mouse_x <= c_7 and mouse_y >= m_6 and mouse_y <= l_4 then
				local value_10 = mouse_x - position_2_2.X;
				local value_11 = mouse_y - position_2_2.Y;
				local huge_2 = value_10 * value_10 + value_11 * value_11;
				if huge_2 < huge then
					huge = huge_2;
					player = player_2;
				end
			end
			if k_2 then
				if self.Toggles.Esp and goodnight.Miscellaneous.Esp.Enabled then
					drawing.Visible = not self.Panic;
					if value_2.Name[1] then
						drawing_2.Visible = drawing.Visible;
					end
				end
			end
			if k_2 and fov["2d-Box"].Visibility == "Only Targetting" and fov.Mode == "2d-Box" and(goodnight["Silent Aim"].Enabled and goodnight["Silent Aim"]["Keybind Mode"].Enabled or goodnight["Silent Aim"]["Set As Aim Assist Target"]) then
				if self.Target["Silent Aim"].Player == player_2 and not drawing.Visible then
					drawing.Visible = not self.Panic;
					if value_2.Name[1] then
						drawing_2.Visible = drawing.Visible;
					end
				end
			end
			if goodnight.Miscellaneous.Esp.Enabled then
				if not goodnight.Miscellaneous.Esp["Override Raid Awareness Visibility"] then
					if k_2 and self["Box Target"].Raid[player_2] then
						drawing.Visible = not self.Panic;
						if value_2.Name[1] then
							drawing_2.Visible = drawing.Visible;
						end
					end
				end
			else
				if k_2 and self["Box Target"].Raid[player_2] then
					drawing.Visible = not self.Panic;
					if value_2.Name[1] then
						drawing_2.Visible = drawing.Visible;
					end
				end
			end
			if fov["2d-Box"].Visibility == "Always on" and(k_2 and fov.Mode == "2d-Box" or fov.Enabled) then
				drawing.Visible = not self.Panic;
				if value_2.Name[1] then
					drawing_2.Visible = drawing.Visible;
				end
			end
			if k_2 or fov.Enabled then
				if(k_2 and fov.Mode == "2d-Box" or fov.Enabled) and fov["2d-Box"].Visibility == "Only Targetting" and player_2 == player then
					drawing.Visible = not self.Panic;
					if value_2.Name[1] then
						drawing_2.Visible = drawing.Visible;
					end
				end
				if not self["Box Target"].Raid[player_2] or feature_name ~= "Silent Aim" then
					if player_2 == player or k_2 and goodnight["Silent Aim"]["Set As Aim Assist Target"] and self.Target["Silent Aim"].Player == player_2 or k_2 and goodnight["Silent Aim"]["Keybind Mode"].Enabled and self.Target["Silent Aim"].Player == player_2 then
						drawing.Color = self.Colors["2d-Box"][feature_name].Targetting;
						drawing_2.Color = drawing.Color;
					else
						drawing.Color = self.Colors["2d-Box"][feature_name].Normal;
						drawing_2.Color = drawing.Color;
					end
				end
			end
		else
			drawing.Visible = false;
			if value_2.Name[1] then
				drawing_2.Visible = drawing.Visible;
			end
		end
	end
	self["Box Target"][feature_name] = player;
end
function application:ThreeDBoxUpdater(value)
	for value_2, value_3 in pairs(self["Box Target"]["3d-Box"].Parts) do
		if goodnight["Silent Aim"].Fov.Mode == "3d-Box" then
			if self.Target["Silent Aim"].Player and value_2 == self.Target["Silent Aim"].Player then
				local transparency = goodnight["Silent Aim"].Fov["3d-Box"].Transparency;
				if value_3.Transparency ~= transparency then
					value_3.Transparency = transparency;
				end
			else
				if value_3.Transparency ~= 1 then
					value_3.Transparency = 1;
				end
			end
		else
			if value_3.Transparency ~= 1 then
				value_3.Transparency = 1;
			end
		end
		local can_query = goodnight["Silent Aim"].Fov.Mode == "3d-Box" and not self.GameInfo.Info.settings["0-delay"].bool and true or false;
		if value_3.CanQuery ~= can_query then
			value_3.CanQuery = can_query;
		end
	end
end
randomize_hit_chance = function(value)
	random_state = (random_state + value % 50) % 5891
	return(2015) * value % 10000 + value * (1702) % 4
end
randomize_spread = function(value)
	random_state = (random_state + value % 35) % 6711
	return(value + (3252)) % 100 * value % ((8453) % 100 + 1)
end
application.__index = application
local stats = game:GetService("Stats")
local ping
for value_, item in stats:GetDescendants() do
	if item.Name == "Data Ping" and item.ClassName == "RunningAverageItemInt" then
		ping = item
		break
	end
end
if not ping and getnilinstances then
	for value__2, item_2 in getnilinstances() do
		if item_2.Name == "Data Ping" and item_2.ClassName == "RunningAverageItemInt" then
			assert(ping == nil, "Multiple detached Data Ping objects; cannot select the original binding")
			ping = item_2
		end
	end
end
ping_stat = ping

application:Init()
return application
